The Parent can lower the EA part (down to 0.01) and the online part updates automatically so both parts add up to the total (TC-051, TC-062).<br>2. Entering 0.00 or an amount above the available balance is refused on SCR-04 with the corresponding validation message (TC-053, TC-057). | The Payment split box renders static read-only text ('Use the full available balance from the Education Account first, then pay the remainder online. Education Account SGD 300.00 / Online Gateway SGD 170.00') with no input control. | TC-051, TC-053, TC-057, TC-062 | Image 10 (sheet Evidence)<br>evidence_10_BUG-R9-010.png | Root cause: Frontend implements split payment as read-only full-balance deduction (i18n 'split.readOnlyDescription') even though backend CheckoutPlanner accepts custom eaAmount. |
| BUG-R9-011 | [API Checkout Split] CheckoutRequestDto ignores onlineAmount and does not reject split requests whose parts sum to less than the invoice total | S1 (highest) | Critical | Open | Functional | SIT (Local QA Stack)<br>e-Service: http://localhost:5174<br>API Gateway: http://localhost:5273<br>Database: edufams-qa-sqlserver (SQL Server 2022)<br>Browser: Chrome (latest), Windows 11<br>Build: QA_BE / develop (22 Oct 2026) | An outstanding charge of SGD 250.00 exists for a student with an active EA balance of SGD 180.00. | 1. Send POST /api/billing/v1/checkout with paymentMethod = 'Both', eaAmount = 100.00, and onlineAmount = 100.00 (sum = 200.00 < 250.00).<br>2. Inspect the HTTP status code and response payload. | The API refuses the request with HTTP 400 Bad Request (SPLIT_AMOUNT_INVALID) because the two parts (100.00 + 100.00 = 200.00) do not add up to the 250.00 amount to pay, and no transaction is created (TC-055). | The API returns HTTP 200 OK and creates a transaction with EA leg = 100.00 and Online leg = 150.00 because CheckoutRequestDto ignores onlineAmount and silently calculates onlineAmount = total - eaAmount. | TC-055 | Image 11 (sheet Evidence)<br>evidence_11_BUG-R9-011.png | Root cause: CheckoutRequestDto has no OnlineAmount property; CheckoutPlanner only validates EaAmount and derives the online leg as Total - EaAmount. |

Open /billing/01999001-0000-7000-8000-000000000001.<br>2. Select a charge (e.g., SGD 75.00), keep Education Account selected on Step 2, and click Continue to Step 3 (SCR-05).<br>3. Check the review summary for the remaining Education Account balance after payment. | SCR-05 shows the child name, invoice/charge details, amount to pay, payment method, and the projected Education Account balance after payment (TC-063). | Step 3 ('Check your payment details') shows 'Education Account SGD 75.00', 'Online Gateway SGD 0.00', and the 'Authorize & pay' button, but does not display the remaining EA balance after payment. | TC-063 | Image 12 (sheet Evidence)<br>evidence_12_BUG-R9-012.png | Root cause: Review step in BillingPaymentPage.tsx does not render (wallet.balance - eaAmount) as 'Balance after payment'. |
| BUG-R9-013 | [Audit Log & Late Webhook] BillingPaymentService has no audit_logs table for F9 payment events and ignores late payment.succeeded webhooks after Failed | S1 (highest) | Critical | Open | Data | SIT (Local QA Stack)<br>e-Service: http://localhost:5174<br>API Gateway: http://localhost:5273<br>Database: edufams-qa-sqlserver (SQL Server 2022)<br>Browser: Chrome (latest), Windows 11<br>Build: QA_BE / develop (22 Oct 2026) | EA payment, Split payment, or OnlineGateway webhook settlement is executed on BillingPaymentService. | 1. Execute an EA payment or Split payment and query BillingPaymentService for audit log records of F9_PAYMENT_INITIATED, F9_PAYMENT_COMPLETED, and EA reversals (TC-071, TC-076, TC-098, TC-167).<br>2. On a failed OnlineGateway transaction (status = 3 Failed), send a late 'payment.succeeded' webhook with a new eventId and inspect the transaction status. | 1. Every payment initiation, leg transition, completion, and EA reversal is written to the audit log in chronological order.<br>2. A late 'payment.succeeded' webhook arriving after a transaction was already marked Failed transitions the transaction to ReconciliationRequired (status = 5) and writes an alert/audit entry. | 1. BillingPaymentService database has 0 audit log tables (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sys.tables WHERE name LIKE '%audit%' returns 0) and writes no audit log entries.<br>2. ProcessGatewayWebhookHandler marks a late 'payment.succeeded' webhook on a Failed transaction as Ignored (receipt status = 3) and leaves transaction.status = 3 (Failed) instead of 5 (ReconciliationRequired). | TC-071, TC-076, TC-098, TC-167 | Image 13 (sheet Evidence)<br>evidence_13_BUG-R9-013.png | Root cause: Audit logging is not implemented in BillingPaymentService, and ProcessGatewayWebhookHandler exits early with WebhookReceiptStatus.Ignored whenever transaction.Status != Pending. |
| BUG-R9-014 | [Checkout EA & Concurrency] CreateCheckoutHandler lacks EA debit compensation/reversal on save error, timeout reconciliation, and Invoice RowVersion concurrency token | S1 (highest) | Critical | Open | Functional | SIT (Local QA Stack)<br>e-Service: http://localhost:5174<br>API Gateway: http://localhost:5273<br>Database: edufams-qa-sqlserver (SQL Server 2022)<br>Browser: Chrome (latest), Windows 11<br>Build: QA_BE / develop (22 Oct 2026) | Parent confirms an EA or Split payment in BillingPaymentService. | 1. Inspect CreateCheckoutHandler.cs handling when EducationAccountClient.DebitAsync returns an unclear/timeout error or when saving the invoice/transaction to SQL Server fails after DebitAsync succeeded (TC-073, TC-074, TC-075).<br>2. Inspect InvoiceModelCreating.cs for optimistic concurrency protection on dbo.invoices.row_version (TC-166). | 1. On unclear EA debit outcome, BillingPaymentService queries Function 1 with the same FundingReference, reverses the debit via CreditAsync if needed, or moves the payment to ReconciliationRequired if reversal fails, showing a safe user message (TC-073, TC-074, TC-075).<br>2. dbo.invoices.row_version is configured as an EF Core concurrency token so concurrent modifications trigger compensation (TC-166). | 1. CreateCheckoutHandler.cs states 'Happy-path only — no reverse/compensation', propagates raw EA client error messages, and has no compensation block calling CreditAsync if DB persistence fails after wallet debit.<br>2. In InvoiceModelCreating.cs (line 61), '// b.Property(i => i.RowVersion).IsRowVersion();' is commented out. | TC-073, TC-074, TC-075, TC-166 | Image 14 (sheet Evidence)<br>evidence_14_BUG-R9-014.png | Root cause: CreateCheckoutHandler was implemented as happy-path only without compensating CreditAsync calls, and RowVersion concurrency was commented out in InvoiceModelCreating.cs. |
| BUG-R9-015 | [EF Core Mapping] dbo.invoices student_id and school_id are uniqueidentifier in SQL Server but mapped as string in C#, throwing HTTP 500 InvalidCastException on GetByIdAsync | S1 (highest) | Critical | Open | Data | SIT (Local QA Stack)<br>e-Service: http://localhost:5174<br>API Gateway: http://localhost:5273<br>Database: edufams-qa-sqlserver (SQL Server 2022)<br>Browser: Chrome (latest), Windows 11<br>Build: QA_BE / develop (22 Oct 2026) | An invoice row exists in BillingPaymentService.dbo.invoices (created by POST /api/billing/v1/checkout). | 1. Create an OnlineGateway or Split (Both) checkout via POST /api/billing/v1/checkout.<br>2. Send a valid signed 'payment.succeeded' webhook to POST /billing/v1/webhooks/payment-gateway (TC-088, TC-089, TC-096, TC-097, TC-115, TC-119, TC-120, TC-143).<br>3. Or resend POST /api/billing/v1/checkout with the exact same Idempotency-Key (up to 100 chars) to replay the first result (TC-078, TC-080). | 1. On 'payment.succeeded' webhook, SettleSucceededAsync loads the invoice via _invoiceRepo.GetByIdAsync, marks the online leg and transaction Succeeded, marks the invoice Paid (4), and returns HTTP 200 OK.<br>2. On idempotent checkout replay with the same Idempotency-Key, CreateCheckoutHandler loads the existing invoice and returns HTTP 200 OK with the original transaction details. | Both flows fail with HTTP 500 Internal Server Error: {"isSuccess":false,"statusCode":500,"message":"Unable to cast object of type 'System.Guid' to type 'System.String'."}. Because SettleSucceededAsync throws before committing, no online or split payment can transition to Succeeded/Paid via webhook. | TC-078, TC-080, TC-088, TC-089, TC-096, TC-097, TC-115, TC-119, TC-120, TC-143 | Image 15 (sheet Evidence)<br>evidence_15_BUG-R9-015.png | Root cause: In SQL Server (BillingPaymentService.dbo.invoices), student_id and school_id are uniqueidentifier (Guid), but Invoice.cs and InvoiceModelCreating.cs map StudentId and SchoolId as string (varchar(50)). Fix: add a HasConversion<Guid>() value converter or align the column/property types. |
| BUG-R9-016 | [API Checkout Validation] Idempotency-Key of 101 characters is not rejected at input validation (returns 404 instead of 400) | S3 | Medium | Open | Functional | SIT (Local QA Stack)<br>e-Service: http://localhost:5174<br>API Gateway: http://localhost:5273<br>Database: edufams-qa-sqlserver (SQL Server 2022)<br>Browser: Chrome (latest), Windows 11<br>Build: QA_BE / develop (22 Oct 2026) | Parent is authenticated and sends a checkout request to POST /api/billing/v1/checkout. | 1. Send POST /api/billing/v1/checkout with idempotencyKey set to a 101-character string ('k' * 101).<br>2. Observe the HTTP response status code. | The request is rejected immediately at input validation with HTTP 400 Bad Request because Idempotency-Key exceeds the 100-character maximum (TC-081). | CheckoutPlanner.ValidateShape does not check IdempotencyKey.Length > 100, so execution proceeds to charge lookup and returns HTTP 404 Not Found ('Charge(s) not found or not outstanding'). | TC-081 | Image 16 (sheet Evidence)<br>evidence_16_BUG-R9-016.png | Root cause: CheckoutPlanner.ValidateShape only checks string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.IdempotencyKey) and omits request.IdempotencyKey.Trim().Length > 100. |
| BUG-R9-017 | [Online Payment Failure / Cancel / Expiry] Failed, cancelled or expired online payment leaves invoice stuck in PaymentPending (3) instead of restoring to Issued (2) | S1 (highest) | Critical | Open | Functional | SIT (Local QA Stack)<br>e-Service: http://localhost:5174<br>API Gateway: http://localhost:5273<br>Database: edufams-qa-sqlserver (SQL Server 2022)<br>Browser: Chrome (latest), Windows 11<br>Build: QA_BE / develop (22 Oct 2026) | An OnlineGateway payment has been initiated (invoice.status = 3 PaymentPending, transaction.status = 1 Pending). | 1. Send a signed 'payment.failed' webhook to POST /billing/v1/webhooks/payment-gateway and inspect dbo.invoices.status and dbo.invoice_items (TC-090, TC-139).<br>2. Send a signed 'payment.cancelled' (TC-091) or 'payment.expired' (TC-092) webhook and inspect dbo.webhook_receipts, dbo.payment_transactions, and dbo.invoices.<br>3. Inspect CreateCheckoutHandler when gateway session creation fails on an OnlineGateway checkout (TC-094). | 1. On 'payment.failed' or 'payment.expired', the online leg and transaction become Failed (3), the invoice returns to Issued (2), and the charge can be selected again on SCR-02 (TC-090, TC-092, TC-139).<br>2. On 'payment.cancelled', the online leg and transaction become Cancelled (4) and the invoice returns to Issued (2) (TC-091).<br>3. When provider session creation fails during checkout, the invoice is not left in PaymentPending (TC-094). | 1. On 'payment.failed', SettleFailed sets transaction.status = 3 (Failed) but leaves dbo.invoices.status = 3 (PaymentPending) and keeps dbo.invoice_items attached, permanently locking the charge from SCR-02.<br>2. On 'payment.cancelled' and 'payment.expired', ProcessGatewayWebhookHandler marks the webhook receipt as Ignored (status = 3) and leaves transaction = 1 (Pending) and invoice = 3 (PaymentPending).<br>3. When gateway session creation fails in CreateCheckoutHandler, invoice.Status remains PaymentPending (3). | TC-090, TC-091, TC-092, TC-094, TC-139 | Image 17 (sheet Evidence)<br>evidence_17_BUG-R9-017.png | Root cause: ProcessGatewayWebhookHandler.SettleFailed does not update invoice.Status back to Issued (2) or release invoice_items, and ProcessGatewayWebhookHandler only handles 'payment.succeeded' and 'payment.failed'. |

Open /payment-result?transactionId={txId} for a ReconciliationRequired split payment where the EA leg is also in ReconciliationRequired (TC-128).<br>3. Read the status banner message. | 1. While pending, SCR-06 states in prose that the Education Account portion (SGD 20.00) has already been taken/deducted while the online part is being confirmed (TC-101).<br>2. While verifying (ReconciliationRequired), SCR-06 states the Education Account portion that was taken and offers no retry (TC-128). | 1. Please contact support if the status does not change.' without naming the EA part taken. | TC-101, TC-128 | Image 18 (sheet Evidence)<br>evidence_18_BUG-R9-018.png | Root cause: PaymentResultPage.tsx uses the same generic pending banner for single and split payments, and only shows the EA-deducted note in ReconciliationRequired when eaLeg.status === 'Succeeded'. |
| BUG-R9-019 | [Split Payment Failure / Cancel / Timeout] Online failure, cancellation or session failure after EA debit does not automatically reverse the EA deduction | S1 (highest) | Critical | Open | Functional | SIT (Local QA Stack)<br>e-Service: http://localhost:5174<br>API Gateway: http://localhost:5273<br>Database: edufams-qa-sqlserver (SQL Server 2022)<br>Browser: Chrome (latest), Windows 11<br>Build: QA_BE / develop (22 Oct 2026) | A Split payment (Both) has debited the student's Education Account wallet (EA leg = 2 Succeeded) and created an OnlineGateway leg (status = 1 Pending). | 1. Trigger an online failure via 'payment.failed' webhook (TC-103, TC-108, TC-109) or parent cancellation via 'payment.cancelled' webhook (TC-104) on a Split transaction.<br>2. Query BillingPaymentService (payment_transactions, payment_funding_legs) and EducationAccountDatabase (wallets).<br>3. Inspect CreateCheckoutHandler.cs when online session creation fails after EA debit (TC-105) or when online session times out (TC-107). | When the online leg of a Split payment fails, is cancelled, fails session creation, or expires, BillingPaymentService calls Function 1 (CreditAsync) to reverse the EA debit, sets the EA leg to Reversed (4) with a ReversalReference, restores the student's wallet balance, marks the transaction Failed/Cancelled, and restores the invoice to Issued (2) (or sets both the EA leg and transaction to ReconciliationRequired (5) if the reversal call itself fails, TC-109). | 1. ProcessGatewayWebhookHandler.SettleFailed never calls EducationAccountClient.CreditAsync; it sets transaction.Status = 5 (ReconciliationRequired) while leaving the EA leg at status = 2 (Succeeded), reversal_reference = NULL, and wallet.balance = 0.00 (unrefunded).<br>2. In CreateCheckoutHandler.cs (line 226), online session creation failure logs 'TODO reverse EA leg' without calling CreditAsync.<br>3. 'payment.cancelled' is ignored completely. | TC-103, TC-104, TC-105, TC-107, TC-108, TC-109 | Image 19 (sheet Evidence)<br>evidence_19_BUG-R9-019.png | Root cause: SettleFailed in ProcessGatewayWebhookHandler.cs and the fallback branch in CreateCheckoutHandler.cs do not invoke _eaClient.CreditAsync to refund the EA wallet on split payment failure. |

| BUG-R9-022 | [Error Handling UI] 5xx API errors on charge list, checkout confirm, and payment result render raw server error strings (SqlException) on screen | S2 | High | Open | Security | SIT (Local QA Stack)<br>e-Service: http://localhost:5174<br>API Gateway: http://localhost:5273<br>Database: edufams-qa-sqlserver (SQL Server 2022)<br>Browser: Chrome (latest), Windows 11<br>Build: QA_BE / develop (22 Oct 2026) | A 500 Internal Server Error with server error details in errors[] occurs when loading outstanding charges (SCR-02), confirming checkout (SCR-05), or loading payment result (SCR-08). | 1. (TC-138, TC-144, TC-164). | getBillingPaymentErrorMessage in packages/api-client/src/api-error.ts concatenates and returns response.errors[] verbatim on 5xx responses, causing the UI to display raw 'System.Data.SqlClient.SqlException...' strings directly to the Parent. | TC-138, TC-144, TC-164 | Image 22 (sheet Evidence)<br>evidence_22_BUG-R9-022.png | Root cause: packages/api-client/src/api-error.ts returns server errors[] strings even when statusCode >= 500 instead of masking 5xx errors with a generic user-safe message. |

Insert a line into dbo.invoice_items linking that student_charge to the invoice (TC-149). | The database (and checkout planner) refuses to attach a student_charge whose school_id differs from the invoice's school_id (TC-149). | The insert into dbo.invoice_items succeeds ('OK') because neither a composite foreign key / trigger in SQL Server nor a check in CheckoutPlanner verifies that student_charges.school_id matches invoices.school_id. | TC-149 | Image 23 (sheet Evidence)<br>evidence_23_BUG-R9-023.png | Root cause: Missing composite FK (invoice_id, school_id) / trigger on dbo.invoice_items and missing multi-school validation in CheckoutPlanner. |
